          objNaN.value = Double.NaN;
          String jsonNaN = gson.toJson(objNaN);
          assertTrue(jsonNaN.contains("NaN"));

          objInfinity.value = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null input for JSON serialization.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with a null object.
      2. The method checks if the input is null and takes the appropriate branch.
    input_focus: |
      Pass null as the argument to the toJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method returns a JSON representation that indicates null.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null input for JSON deserialization.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Class<T> classOfT)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Class<T> classOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a null JSON string.
      2. The method checks if the input JSON is null and takes the appropriate branch.
    input_focus: |
      Pass null as the argument to the fromJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the method returns null without throwing an exception.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when JSON input is empty.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(JsonReader reader, Type typeOfT)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(Reader json, Type typeOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with an empty JSON string.
      2. The method checks if the input is empty and handles it accordingly.
    input_focus: |
      Use an empty string as the argument to the fromJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method returns null or an appropriate default value without throwing an exception.

  - design_name: |
      Test generation of non-executable JSON.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public JsonWriter newJsonWriter(Writer writer)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object while the generateNonExecutableJson flag is true.
      2. The method generates a JSON writer that includes a prefix for non-executable JSON.
    input_focus: |
      Set the generateNonExecutableJson flag to true and pass a valid object to the toJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the returned JSON string starts with the non-executable prefix.

  - design_name: |
      Test pretty printing of JSON output.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public JsonWriter newJsonWriter(Writer writer)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object while the prettyPrinting flag is true.
      2. The method generates a JSON writer that formats the output with indentation.
    input_focus: |
      Set the prettyPrinting flag to true and pass a valid object to the toJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the returned JSON string is formatted with proper indentation and line breaks.
```

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when delegate is null during type adapter retrieval.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void setDelegate(TypeAdapter<T> typeAdapter)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a TypeToken for a type that has no registered adapter.
      2. Inside getAdapter(), the setDelegate() method is invoked.
      3. The branch checking if delegate is null is reached.
    input_focus: |
      Use a TypeToken for a type that does not have a corresponding TypeAdapter registered.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalStateException is thrown indicating that the delegate is not set.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values in double serialization.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> doubleAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src, Type typeOfSrc)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object that has a double field set to null.
      2. Inside toJson(), doubleAdapter() is called to get the appropriate adapter.
      3. The branch checking if the value is null in the write method is reached.
    input_focus: |
      Construct an object with a double field explicitly set to null.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the JSON output correctly represents the null value for the double field.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when special floating-point values are serialized.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> floatAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src, Type typeOfSrc)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object containing a float field set to NaN or Infinity.
      2. Inside toJson(), floatAdapter() is called to get the appropriate adapter.
      3. The branch checking if serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues is true is reached.
    input_focus: |
      Create an object with a float field set to Float.NaN or Float.POSITIVE_INFINITY.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the JSON output correctly represents the special floating-point value.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of invalid double values during serialization.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        static void checkValidFloatingPoint(double value)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src, Type typeOfSrc)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object that has a double field set to NaN or Infinity.
      2. Inside toJson(), checkValidFloatingPoint() is invoked for the double value.
      3. The branch checking for invalid double values is reached.
    input_focus: |
      Construct an object with a double field set to Double.NaN or Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown indicating the double value is invalid.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when long serialization policy is not default.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> longAdapter(LongSerializationPolicy longSerializationPolicy)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src, Type typeOfSrc)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object that has a long field.
      2. Inside toJson(), longAdapter() is called with a non-default LongSerializationPolicy.
      3. The branch checking if longSerializationPolicy is DEFAULT is reached.
    input_focus: |
      Create an object with a long field and set the serialization policy to a non-default value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the JSON output reflects the serialization behavior defined by the non-default policy.
```

            }

        futureAdapter.setDelegate(adapter1);
        // Attempt to set a second delegate
        TypeAdapter<String> adapter2 = new TypeAdapter<String>() {
            @Override
            public String read(JsonReader in) throws IOException {
                return in.nextString();
            }
            @Override
            public void write(JsonWriter out, String value) throws IOException {
                out.value(value);
            }
        };
        futureAdapter.setDelegate(adapter2); // This should throw an AssertionError
    }
  new_imports_code: |
    import com.google.gson.TypeAdapter;
    import com.google.gson.stream.JsonReader;
    import com.google.gson.stream.JsonWriter;

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values during serialization.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> doubleAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with a null object.
      2. The doubleAdapter(...) method is reached, and the null check branch is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Pass a null value to the toJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output is a JSON representation of null.

  - design_name: |
      Test exception thrown for invalid double values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> doubleAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object that contains a field with a NaN or infinite double value.
      2. The doubleAdapter(...) method is reached, and the invalid value check branch is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Create an object with a field set to Double.NaN or Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown indicating the invalid double value.

  - design_name: |
      Test serialization of AtomicLongArray.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<AtomicLongArray> atomicLongArrayAdapter(final TypeAdapter<Number> longAdapter)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an AtomicLongArray object.
      2. The atomicLongArrayAdapter(...) method is reached, and the serialization loop is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an AtomicLongArray initialized with several long values.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the serialized JSON output correctly represents the array's contents.

  - design_name: |
      Test retrieval of type adapter for null key surrogate.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(Class<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a null TypeToken.
      2. The getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type) method is reached, and the null key surrogate branch is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Use a null value as the argument for getAdapter().
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the returned TypeAdapter is the one associated with the NULL_KEY_SURROGATE.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when type adapter factory is skipped.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getDelegateAdapter(TypeAdapterFactory skipPast, TypeToken<T> type)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a TypeToken that requires a specific type adapter.
      2. The getDelegateAdapter(...) method is reached, and the factory skipping logic is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Register a type adapter factory and pass a TypeToken that should skip the registered factory.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the correct type adapter is returned, bypassing the skipped factory.
```

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a null source object is passed to toJson().
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void toJson(Object src, Type typeOfSrc, Appendable writer)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with a null object.
      2. The null check branch is taken, leading to a call to toJson(JsonNull.INSTANCE).
    input_focus: |
      Pass null as the argument to the toJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output is a valid JSON representation of null.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a non-empty JSON document is not fully consumed.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private static void assertFullConsumption(Object obj, JsonReader reader)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(JsonReader reader, Type typeOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a JsonReader that has additional data after a valid object.
      2. The assertFullConsumption() method is reached, checking for remaining tokens.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a JsonReader that reads a valid JSON object followed by extra data.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that a JsonIOException is thrown indicating the JSON document was not fully consumed.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a null JSON element is passed to fromJson().
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private <T> T fromJson(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Type typeOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a null JSON element.
      2. The null check branch is taken, returning null.
    input_focus: |
      Pass a null value as the argument to the fromJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the return value is null.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a delegate is set multiple times in FutureTypeAdapter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public void setDelegate(TypeAdapter<T> typeAdapter)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T read(JsonReader in)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Create a FutureTypeAdapter instance.
      2. Call setDelegate() twice with different TypeAdapters.
      3. The second call should trigger an AssertionError.
    input_focus: |
      Use a FutureTypeAdapter and attempt to set a delegate twice.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that an AssertionError is thrown on the second delegate set attempt.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a null delegate is accessed in FutureTypeAdapter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public T read(JsonReader in)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a TypeToken that has not been set up.
      2. The read() method of FutureTypeAdapter is reached, accessing the null delegate.
    input_focus: |
      Use a TypeToken that does not have an associated TypeAdapter.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalStateException is thrown due to the null delegate.
```

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values during JSON writing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> doubleAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object that contains a field with a null value.
      2. During serialization, doubleAdapter(...) is reached and the null value branch is taken.
      3. The null value is handled according to the serialization settings.
    input_focus: |
      Create an object with a field that is explicitly set to null.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the resulting JSON output correctly represents the null field according to the serialization rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values during JSON reading.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> floatAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Class<T> classOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a JSON string that includes a null value for a field.
      2. During deserialization, floatAdapter(...) is reached and the null value branch is taken.
      3. The null value is processed and assigned to the corresponding field in the object.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a JSON string that includes a null value for a specific field.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the deserialized object has the corresponding field set to null.

  - design_name: |
      Test exception handling for invalid floating-point values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        static void checkValidFloatingPoint(double value)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object that has a field with an invalid floating-point value (NaN or Infinity).
      2. During serialization, checkValidFloatingPoint(...) is reached and the invalid value is processed.
      3. An exception is thrown due to the invalid value.
    input_focus: |
      Create an object with a field that contains NaN or Infinity as its value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the appropriate exception is thrown when attempting to serialize the object.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when using a custom long serialization policy.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> longAdapter(LongSerializationPolicy longSerializationPolicy)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object that has a long field.
      2. During serialization, longAdapter(...) is reached and the branch for a custom serialization policy is taken.
      3. The long value is serialized according to the specified policy.
    input_focus: |
      Create an object with a long field and specify a custom LongSerializationPolicy.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the serialized output reflects the custom serialization policy applied to the long value.

  - design_name: |
      Test retrieval of a type adapter for a specific type.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Type typeOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a JSON string and a specific TypeToken for a custom type.
      2. During deserialization, getAdapter(...) is reached to retrieve the appropriate type adapter.
      3. The type adapter is used to deserialize the JSON into the specified type.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a JSON string that matches the structure of the custom type and a corresponding TypeToken.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the deserialized object matches the expected structure and type as defined by the TypeToken.
```

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null input in toJson() method.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with a null object.
      2. The method checks for null and calls toJson(JsonNull.INSTANCE).
    input_focus: |
      Pass null as the argument to the toJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the returned JSON string represents a null value correctly.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a non-empty JSON document is not fully consumed.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private static void assertFullConsumption(Object obj, JsonReader reader)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(JsonReader reader, Type typeOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a JsonReader that has extra data after a valid object.
      2. The assertFullConsumption() method is reached and checks for remaining tokens.
    input_focus: |
      Create a JsonReader with a valid JSON object followed by additional unexpected data.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that a JsonIOException is thrown indicating the JSON document was not fully consumed.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a TypeAdapterFactory is not found.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(Class<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a type that has no registered TypeAdapter.
      2. The method attempts to find a suitable TypeAdapter and fails.
    input_focus: |
      Use a Class type that is not supported by any registered TypeAdapter.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown indicating that Gson cannot handle the type.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a TypeAdapter is overridden by a registered factory.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getDelegateAdapter(TypeAdapterFactory skipPast, TypeToken<T> type)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a type that has a registered TypeAdapterFactory.
      2. The getDelegateAdapter() method is reached and checks for the skipPast factory.
    input_focus: |
      Register a TypeAdapterFactory that overrides the default behavior for a specific type.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the returned TypeAdapter reflects the behavior defined in the registered factory.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a null value is serialized in a JSON object.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private TypeAdapter<Number> doubleAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with an object containing a null field.
      2. The doubleAdapter() method is reached and checks for null values during serialization.
    input_focus: |
      Create an object with a field that is explicitly set to null.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the serialized JSON omits the null field according to the serialization rules.
```

language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testToJsonWithNullInput()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test handling of null input in toJson() method.
    test_name: testToJsonWithNullInput
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testToJsonWithNullInput() {
          Gson gson = new Gson();
          String json = gson.toJson(null);
          assertEquals("null", json);
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a non-empty JSON document is not fully consumed.
    test_name: testFromJsonWithExtraData
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testFromJsonWithExtraData() {
          Gson gson = new Gson();
          String json = "{\"name\":\"test\"} extra data"; // Valid JSON followed by extra data
          JsonReader reader = new JsonReader(new StringReader(json));
          try {
              gson.fromJson(reader, Object.class);
              fail("Expected JsonIOException due to incomplete consumption");
          } catch (JsonIOException e) {
              // Expected exception
          }
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a TypeAdapterFactory is not found.
    test_name: testGetAdapterWithUnknownType
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testGetAdapterWithUnknownType() {
          Gson gson = new Gson();
          try {
              gson.getAdapter(new TypeToken<Object>() {}); // Using Object which has no registered TypeAdapter
              fail("Expected IllegalArgumentException due to unknown type adapter");
          } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
              assertTrue(e.getMessage().contains("GSON cannot handle"));
          }
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a TypeAdapter is overridden by a registered factory.
    test_name: testGetDelegateAdapterWithRegisteredFactory
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testGetDelegateAdapterWithRegisteredFactory() {
          Gson gson = new Gson();
          TypeAdapterFactory factory = new CustomTypeAdapterFactory(); // Custom factory that does nothing
          TypeAdapter<String> adapter = gson.getDelegateAdapter(factory, TypeToken.get(String.class));
          assertNotNull(adapter);
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a null value is serialized in a JSON object.
    test_name: testToJsonWithObjectContainingNullField
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testToJsonWithObjectContainingNullField() {
          class TestObject {
              Double value = null; // Field explicitly set to null
          }
          Gson gson = new Gson();
          String json = gson.toJson(new TestObject());
          assertFalse(json.contains("value")); // The null field should be omitted
          assertEquals("{}", json); // The result should be an empty JSON object
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a delegate is not set in FutureTypeAdapter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private T read(JsonReader in)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a TypeToken that does not have a registered adapter.
      2. Inside getAdapter(), a FutureTypeAdapter is created and its read() method is called.
      3. The read() method checks if the delegate is null, triggering the uncovered branch.
    input_focus: |
      Use a TypeToken for a type that has not been registered with any TypeAdapter.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalStateException is thrown due to the absence of a delegate.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values during JSON reading for Double.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private Double read(JsonReader in)
      entry_hint: |
        public TypeAdapter<Number> doubleAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call doubleAdapter() with serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues set to false.
      2. Inside doubleAdapter(), read() is called on the returned TypeAdapter.
      3. The read() method checks if the input JSON token is null, triggering the uncovered branch.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a JsonReader that points to a JSON null value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the read() method returns null without throwing an exception.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values during JSON writing for Float.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void write(JsonWriter out, Number value)
      entry_hint: |
        public TypeAdapter<Number> floatAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call floatAdapter() with serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues set to false.
      2. Inside floatAdapter(), write() is called with a null value.
      3. The write() method checks if the value is null, triggering the uncovered branch.
    input_focus: |
      Use a TypeAdapter for Float and pass a null value to the write() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the write() method correctly writes a null value to the JsonWriter.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a null value is passed to the longAdapter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private Number read(JsonReader in)
      entry_hint: |
        public TypeAdapter<Number> longAdapter(LongSerializationPolicy longSerializationPolicy)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call longAdapter() with LongSerializationPolicy.DEFAULT.
      2. Inside longAdapter(), read() is called on the returned TypeAdapter.
      3. The read() method checks if the input JSON token is null, triggering the uncovered branch.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a JsonReader that points to a JSON null value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the read() method returns null without throwing an exception.

  - design_name: |
      Test validation of floating point values in checkValidFloatingPoint.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        static void checkValidFloatingPoint(double value)
      entry_hint: |
        public TypeAdapter<Number> doubleAdapter(boolean serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call doubleAdapter() with serializeSpecialFloatingPointValues set to false.
      2. Inside doubleAdapter(), a valid double value is passed to checkValidFloatingPoint.
      3. The method checks for NaN or infinite values, triggering the uncovered branch.
    input_focus: |
      Pass a NaN or infinite double value to checkValidFloatingPoint.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown for invalid double values.
```

      @Test
      public void testSetDelegateInFutureTypeAdapter() {
          FutureTypeAdapter<String> futureTypeAdapter = new FutureTypeAdapter<>();
          TypeAdapter<String> adapter = new TypeAdapter<String>() {
              @Override
              public String read(JsonReader in) throws IOException {
                  return in.nextString();
              }
              @Override
              public void write(JsonWriter out, String value) throws IOException {
                  out.value(value);
              }
          };
          futureTypeAdapter.setDelegate(adapter);
          assertNotNull(adapter); // Ensure delegate is set
      }
    new_imports_code: ""

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values during JSON reading.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private T fromJson(JsonReader reader, Type typeOfT)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Type typeOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a JSON string that represents a null value.
      2. The fromJson(...) method is reached, leading to the JSON reader processing the input.
      3. The branch handling null values is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a JSON string that is either "null" or an empty document.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method returns null without throwing exceptions.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a TypeAdapter is not found.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Type typeOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with a JSON string and a type that has no registered TypeAdapter.
      2. The getAdapter(...) method is invoked to retrieve the TypeAdapter for the specified type.
      3. The branch handling the absence of a TypeAdapter is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Use a type that is not supported by any registered TypeAdapter.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown indicating that the type cannot be handled.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values during JSON writing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void write(JsonWriter out, T value)
      entry_hint: |
        public String toJson(Object src)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call toJson() with a null object.
      2. The write(...) method is reached, where the JSON writer processes the input.
      3. The branch handling null values is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Pass null as the argument to the toJson() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output is a JSON representation of null without throwing exceptions.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a TypeAdapterFactory is skipped.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private <T> TypeAdapter<T> getDelegateAdapter(TypeAdapterFactory skipPast, TypeToken<T> type)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> TypeAdapter<T> getAdapter(TypeToken<T> type)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getAdapter() with a TypeToken that requires a specific TypeAdapterFactory.
      2. The getDelegateAdapter(...) method is invoked, where the skipPast logic is executed.
      3. The branch for when the skipPast factory is not found is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Use a TypeAdapterFactory that is not registered and ensure it is passed as skipPast.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the correct TypeAdapter is returned based on the remaining factories.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of empty JSON documents.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private <T> T fromJson(JsonReader reader, Type typeOfT)
      entry_hint: |
        public <T> T fromJson(String json, Class<T> classOfT)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call fromJson() with an empty JSON string.
      2. The fromJson(...) method is reached, leading to the JSON reader processing the input.
      3. The branch handling empty documents is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an empty JSON string as input.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method returns null without throwing exceptions.
```

  test_name: |
    testGetAdapterWithExistingDelegate
  test_code: |
    @Test
    public void testGetAdapterWithExistingDelegate() {
        Gson gson = new Gson();
        TypeAdapter<String> adapter = gson.getAdapter(String.class);
        // Calling getAdapter again should not throw an error, as the adapter is already cached.
        TypeAdapter<String> sameAdapter = gson.getAdapter(String.class);
        assertSame(adapter, sameAdapter);
    }

    Fix a test that expected an IllegalStateException when calling getDelegateAdapter without a registered factory.
  test_name: |
    testGetDelegateAdapterWithoutRegisteredFactory
  test_code: |
    @Test
    public void testGetDelegateAdapterWithoutRegisteredFactory() {
        Gson gson = new Gson();
        TypeAdapterFactory unregisteredFactory = new CustomTypeAdapterFactory();
        // This should not throw an exception; it should return a valid adapter.
        TypeAdapter<String> adapter = gson.getDelegateAdapter(unregisteredFactory, TypeToken.get(String.class));
        assertNotNull(adapter);
    }
  new_imports_code: |
    import static org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull;
- test_behavior: |
    Fix a test that expected a JsonIOException due to incomplete consumption of JSON.
  test_name: |
    testFromJsonWithPartiallyReadJsonDifferent
  test_code: |
    @Test
    public void testFromJsonWithPartiallyReadJsonDifferent() {
        Gson gson = new Gson();
        String json = "{\"name\":\"test\""; // Incomplete JSON
        JsonReader reader = new JsonReader(new StringReader(json));
        try {
            gson.fromJson(reader, Object.class);
            fail("Expected JsonIOException due to incomplete consumption");
        } catch (JsonIOException e) {
            // Expected exception
        } catch (Exception e) {
            fail("Expected JsonIOException but got: " + e.getClass().getSimpleName());
        }
    }
  new_imports_code: |
    import com.google.gson.JsonIOException;
    import com.google.gson.stream.JsonReader;
    import java.io.StringReader;
